QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char**      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n                 const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\\n    \\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Main clustering loop\\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\\n        int best_seed =   \\n    // Check each cluster\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\\n        \\n        // Check                 const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if Use OpenMP additionally.\n\nLet me design GPU kernel more concretely.\n\nKernel `generateClustersKernel`:\nInputs:\n- const double* xs, ys (or struct Point* points) device.\n- int N\n- const unsigned char* clustered (device, global, N bytes) — read-only during kernel (state at iteration start).\n- double threshold2\n- const int* seeds (device) length S (seeds assigned to this rank)\n- int* out_card (device) length S\n- (optional) members output for recompute: int* out_members (S x N) too big; we recompute separately.\n\nGrid: S blocks; blockDim e.g. 256 threads.\nDynamic shared: layout: int members[N] (but we only need up to N) + unsigned int in_cluster_words[(N+31)/32] + reduction scratch. For reduction, we can use shared arrays of blockDim for candidate best values/indices, or use warp shuffles.\n\nAlgorithm per block:\n```\nextern __shared__ int smem[];\nint* members = smem;                 // N ints\nunsigned int* in_cluster = (unsigned int*)(members + N); // words\nint* s_cand = ...; // maybe blockDim ints for reduction\ndouble* s_val = ...;\nint s_size; // number of members\nint seed = seeds[blockIdx.x];\n// init in_cluster zeros\nfor i in tid..words step blockDim: in_cluster[i]=0;\nif tid==0 { members[0]=seed; s_size=1; set bit seed; }\n__syncthreads();\nwhile (s_size < N) {\n   int M = s_size;\n   // local best per thread\n   double bestv = threshold2; // accept only < threshold2; use min. Initialize to threshold2\n   int bestc = -1;\n   for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n      if (clustered[c]) continue;\n      if (in_cluster[c>>5] & (1u<<(c&31))) continue;\n      double maxd2 = 0.0;\n      for (int j=0;j<M;j++) {  // maybe unroll\n          int m = members[j];\n          double dx = xs[c]-xs[m]; double dy=ys[c]-ys[m];\n          double d2=dx*dx+dy*dy;\n          if (d2>maxd2) maxd2=d2;\n      }\n      if (maxd2 < bestv) { bestv=maxd2; bestc=c; }\n      // Note original: accept if maxd2<threshold2 AND maxd2<min. Since bestv starts threshold2, only < threshold2 accepted. Tie: strict < keeps smaller c within thread (c increasing) and across threads we pick smallest c on equal values.\n   }\n   // reduce: find min bestv; tie smallest bestc. Need handle bestc==-1.\n   // Write to shared and tree-reduce.\n   ...\n   if tid==0 { if (bestc>=0) { members[s_size]=bestc; set bit; s_size++; } else done=true; }\n   __syncthreads();\n   if done break;\n}\nif tid==0 out_card[blockIdx.x] = s_size;\n```\n\nTie-break reduction: Among threads, find candidate with min bestv; if equal, min bestc. Implement:\n```\ns_val[tid]=bestv; s_idx[tid]=bestc;\n__syncthreads();\nfor (stride=blockDim/2; stride>0; stride>>=1) {\n  if (tid<stride) {\n    double v1=s_val[tid], v2=s_val[tid+stride];\n    int i1=s_idx[tid], i2=s_idx[tid+stride];\n    if (i2>=0 && (i1<0 || v2<v1 || (v2==v1 && i2<i1))) { s_val[tid]=v2; s_idx[tid]=i2; }\n  }\n  __syncthreads();\n}\n```\nCompute all threads have bestc maybe -1; if bestv=threshold2. When bestc>=0, bestv<threshold2. Fine.\n\nImportant floating exactness: max computed in order j=0..M-1. Same as CPU original (which iterates members in order). d2 = dx*dx+dy*dy; original sqrt. The candidate seeds of each clustering\n//               round are distributed across ranks; a single packed integer\n//               all-reduce elects the globally best seed every round.\n//   * CUDA    : candidate cluster cardinalities are evaluated on the GPU with\n//               one CUDA block per seed (dynamic work queue).  Each block\n//               maintains the running maximum squared distance from every\n//               unclustered point to the cluster, turning each greedy step\n//               into an O(U) scan/update instead of O(U * |cluster|).\n//   * OpenMP  : the host concurrently evaluates a share of the seeds while the\n//               GPU is busy, and parallelizes the round reduction and the\n//               result validation.  The CPU/GPU seed split is feedback-tuned.\n//\n// The algorithm is numerically identical to the sequential reference: the\n// same squared Euclidean distances are computed, only maxima (which are exact\n// and order independent) and strict \"less than\" comparisons are used.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <numeric>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA device state\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstatic double* g_dxs = nullptr;\nstatic double* g_dys = nullptr;\nstatic int* g_dactive = nullptr;    // full list of unclustered point indices\nstatic int* g_dcards = nullptr;     // per-seed cardinalities\nstatic int* g_dcounter = nullptr;   // dynamic work queue counter\nstatic double* g_dmaxd = nullptr;   // per-block running max distance (fallback)\nstatic size_t g_maxd_capacity = 0;  // doubles allocated in g_dmaxd\nstatic bool g_cuda = false;\nstatic int g_block = 256;\nstatic int g_sm_count = 0;\nstatic size_t g_shared_optin = 0;\n\n// Compute the maximum squared distance from each active point to the growing\n// cluster, one CUDA block per seed.  Blocks pull seeds from a shared atomic\n// work queue so that long/short candidate clusters are load balanced.\n__global__ void computeCardsKernel(const double* __restrict__ xs,\n                                   const double* __restrict__ ys,\n                                   const int* __restrict__ active,\n                                   int U,\n                                   int N,\n                                   double threshold2,\n                                   int posBase,\n                                   int posStride,\n                                   int localStart,\n                                   int S,\n                                   int* __restrict__ cards,\n                                   int* __restrict__ counter,\n                                   double* __restrict__ globalMaxd,\n                                   int useShared) {\n    extern __shared__ __align__(16) unsigned char smem_raw[];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int bd = blockDim.x;\n    const int words = (N + 31) >> 5;\n\n    double* s_val = reinterpret_cast<double*>(smem_raw);          // bd doubles\n    double* s_max = s_val + bd;                                   // U doubles (shared mode)\n    int* s_int = reinterpret_cast<int*>(s_max + (useShared ? U : 0));\n    int* s_idx = s_int;                                           // bd ints\n    int* s_bits = s_idx + bd;                                     // words ints\n    int* s_size = s_bits + words; w < words; w += bd) s_bits[w] = 0;\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            s_bits[seed >> 5] |= (1u << (seed & 31));\n            *s_size = 1;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const double xseed = xs[seed];\n        const double yseed = ys[seed];\n        for (int p = tid; Used for the CPU share of the work and for\n// materializing the elected cluster's member list.\nstatic int generateCandidateCPU(int seed,\n                                int U,\n                                const int* active,\n                                const double* xs,\n                                const double* ys,\n                                double threshold2,\n                                int N,\n                                uint32_t* bits,\n                                double* maxd,\n                                std::vector<int>* members) {\n    const int words = (N + 31) >> 5;\n    std::memset(bits, 0, static_cast<size_t>(words) * sizeof(uint32_t));\n    bits[seed >> 5] |= (1u << (seed & 31));\n\n    if (members) {\n        members->clear();\n        members->push_back(seed);\n    }\n\n    const double xseed = xs[seed];\n    const double yseed = ys[seed];\n    for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n        const int c = active[p];\n        const double dx = xs[c] - xseed;\n        const double dy = ys[c] - yseed;\n        maxd[p] = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n    }\n\n    int size = 1;\n    while (size < U) {\n        double bestv = threshold2;\n        int bestc = -1;\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            const int c = active[p];\n            if (bits[c >> 5] & (1u << (c & 31))) continue;\n            const double v = maxd[p];\n            if (v < bestv) {\n                bestv = v;\n                bestc = c;\n            }\n        }\n        if (bestc < 0) break;\n\n        bits[bestc >> 5] |= (1u << (bestc & 31));\n        ++size;\n        if (members) members->push_back(bestc);\n        if (size >= U) break;\n\n        const double xm = xs[bestc];\n        const double ym = ys[bestc];\n        for (int p = 0; p < U; ++p) {\n            const int c = active[p];\n            if (bits[c >> 5] & (1u << (c & 31))) continue;\n            const double dx = xs[c] - xm;\n            const double dy = ys[c] - ym;\n            const double d = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            if (d > maxd[p]) maxd[p] = d;\n        }\n    }\n    return size;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  MPI_Comm comm,\n                                  int rank,\n                                  int nranks,\n                                  int omp_threads) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    const int words = (N + 31) >> 5;\n    const double threshold2 = threshold * threshold;\n\n    std::vector<uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> active(N);\n    std::iota(active.begin(), active.end(), 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Host point arrays (SoA layout gives the CPU path sequential access).\n    std::vector<double> xs(N), ys(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        xs[i] = points[i].x;\n        ys[i] = points[i].y;\n    }\n\n    // Upload the immutable point coordinates once.\n    if (g_cuda) {\n        cudaMemcpy(g_dxs, xs.data(), (size_t)N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice);\n        cudaMemcpy(g_dys, ys.data(), (size_t)N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice);\n    }\n\n    // Per-thread CPU scratch (max clamp size U <= N).\n    const int T = std::max(1, omp_threads);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> tbits((size_t)T * words);\n    std::vector<double> tmaxd((size_t)T * N);\n\n    std::vector<int> gpu_cards(N);\n    std::vector<int> cpu_cards(N);\n\n    // Feedback-tuned CPU/GPU cost per seed (seconds).\n    double gpu_cost = 1.0;\n    double cpu_cost = 1.0;\n\n    cudaEvent_t ev_start = nullptr, ev_stop = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n    if (g_cuda) {\n        cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking);\n        cudaEventCreate(&ev_start);\n        cudaEventCreate(&ev_stop);\n    }\n\n    while (!active.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(active.size());\n\n        // Contiguous/strided seed partition across ranks.\n        // Rank r owns local indices i with position = r + i * nranks.\n        const int locCount = (U > rank) ? (U - rank + nranks - 1) / nranks : 0;\n\n        int G = 0;\n        if (g_cuda && locCount > 0) {\n            double frac = cpu_cost / (gpu_cost + cpu_cost);\n            G = static_cast<int>(std::llround(static_cast<double>(locCount) * frac));\n            if (G < 0) G = 0;\n            if (G > locCount) G = locCount;\n            if (locCount >= 8) {\n                if (G < 1) G = 1;\n                if (G > locCount - 1) G = locCount - 1;\n            }\n        }\n        const int C = locCount - G;\n\n        double gpu_ms = 0.0;\n        double cpu_dt = 0.0;\n\n        if (G > 0) {\n            // Upload the full unclustered list; all ranks use identical data.\n            cudaMemcpy(g_dactive, active.data(), (size_t)U * sizeof(int),\n                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice);\n            cudaMemsetAsync(g_dcounter, 0, sizeof(int), stream);\n\n            const int wordsN = (N + 31) >> 5;\n            const size_t doublesShared = static_cast<size_t>(U) + g_block;\n            size_t sharedBytes = doublesShared * sizeof(double) +\n                                 static_cast<size_t>(wordsN + g_block + 2) * sizeof(int);\n            size_t smallShared = static_cast<size_t>(g_block) * sizeof(double) +\n                                 static_cast<size_t>(wordsN + g_block + 2) * sizeof(int);\n\n            int useShared = (sharedBytes <= g_shared_optin) ? 1 : 0;\n            if (!useShared) sharedBytes = smallShared;\n\n            int maxBlocks = 1;\n            cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(\n                &maxBlocks, computeCardsKernel, g_block,\n                static_cast<int>(sharedBytes));\n            if (maxBlocks < 1) maxBlocks = 1;\n\n            int grid = g_sm_count * maxBlocks;\n static_cast<int>(budget / ((size_t)U * sizeof(double) + 1));\n                if (cap < 1) cap = 1;\n                if (grid > cap) grid = cap;\n            }\n            if (grid > G) grid = G;\n         }\n            }\n\n            cudaEventRecord(ev_start, stream);\n            computeCardsKernel<<<grid, g_block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(\n                g_dxs, g_dys, g_dactive, U, N, threshold2,\n                rank, nranks, 0, G,\n                g_dcards, g_dcounter, g_dmaxd, useShared);\n            cudaEventRecord(ev_stop, stream);\n        }\n\n        // CPU share runs concurrently with the GPU kernel.\n        if (C > 0) {\n            const double t0 = omp_get_wtime();\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int j = 0; j < C; ++j) {\n                const int t = omp_get_thread_num();\n                const int pos = rank + (G + j) * nranks;\n                cpu_cards[j] = generateCandidateCPU(\n                    active[pos], U, cpu_dt = omp_get_wtime() - t0;\n        }\n\n        if (G > 0) {\n            cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);\n            cudaEventElapsedTime(&gpu_ms, ev_start, ev_stop);\n            cudaMemcpy(gpu_cards.data(), g_dcards, (size_t)G * sizeof(int),\n                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost);\n            if (G >= 1) gpu_cost = 0.5 * gpu_cost + 0.5 * (gpu_ms / 1000.0) / G;\n        }\n        if (C > 0) {\n            cpu_cost = 0.5 * cpu_cost + 0.5 * cpu_dt / C;\n        }\n\n        // Elect the globally best seed: largest cardinality, lowest position\n        // on ties (matching the sequential \"first maximum wins\" rule).\n        uint64_t local_key = 0;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(max : local_key) schedule(static)\n        for (int i = 0; i < locCount; ++i) {\n            const int card = (i < G) ? gpu_cards[i] : cpu_cards[i - G];\n            const int pos = rank + i * nranks;\n            const uint64_t k = (static_cast<uint64_t>(static_cast<uint32_t>(card)) << 32) |\n                               static_cast<uint32_t>(U - 1 - pos);\n            if (k > local_key) local_key = k;\n        }\n\n        uint64_t global_key = 0;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, comm);\n\n        const int best_pos = U - 1 - static_cast<int>(global_key & 0xffffffffu);\n        const int best_seed = active[best_pos];\n\n        // Materialize the elected cluster once on the root, then broadcast.\n        std::vector<int> best_members;\n        int member_count = 0;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            best_members.reserve(N);\n            generateCandidateCPU(best_seed, U, active.data(), xs.data(), ys.data(),\n                                 threshold2, N, tbits.data(), tmaxd.data(),\n                                 &best_members);\n            member_count = static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1,
